Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in mol_bio theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 23 and the invariant I(A,B) = 620 mod 2.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 23 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the mol_bio structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 620. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
